index e69de29..f13848d 100644
--- a/2_test_forward.py

+
+from pathlib import Path
+import torch
+from torchvision.utils import save_image
+
+from hsi_dataset import CaveDataset
+from src.hsi_camera import HSICamera
+
+# 1. 设置 GPU
+device = torch.device(
+    "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
+)
+print("计算设备:", device, flush=True)
+
+# 2. 检查测试数据
+dataset_list = Path("datasets/cave_val.txt")
+scene_path = Path(
+    dataset_list.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()[0]
+)
+png_files = sorted(scene_path.glob("*.png"))
+
+assert scene_path.is_dir(), f"场景目录不存在: {scene_path}"
+assert len(png_files) == 31, (
+    f"期望 31 个波段，实际 {len(png_files)} 个"
+)
+print("测试场景:", scene_path)
+
+# 3. 加载 CAVE 高光谱图像
+dataset = CaveDataset(
+    file_path=str(dataset_list),
+    crop_size=(256, 256),
+    is_train=False
+)
+
+sample = dataset[0]
+
+# 增加 batch 维度
+data_dict = {
+    "wvln": sample["wvln"].unsqueeze(0),
+    "img": sample["img"].unsqueeze(0),
+}
+
+print("高光谱图像:", data_dict["img"].shape)
+print("波长数量:", data_dict["wvln"].shape[1])
+
+# 4. 初始化高光谱相机
+camera = HSICamera(
+    lens_file="./lenses/paraxiallens/doelens_hsi.json",
+    sensor_file="./sensors/flir/BFS-U3-200S7C-C.json",
+    device=device,
+)
+
+# 5. DOE 前向成像
+# 31 个波长都需要计算 PSF，可能耗时数分钟
+print("开始计算 31 波段 DOE 前向成像...", flush=True)
+
+with torch.no_grad():
+    encoded_rgb, spectral_gt = camera.render(data_dict)
+
+    # 没有 DOE 模糊的参考 RGB 图像
+    reference_rgb = camera.spectral2rgb(spectral_gt)
+
+print("编码图像:", encoded_rgb.shape)
+print("真实光谱:", spectral_gt.shape)
+
+for name, img in [
+    ("Ground Truth HSI", spectral_gt),
+    ("Reference RGB", reference_rgb),
+    ("Encoded RGB", encoded_rgb),
+]:
+    print(f"\n{name}")
+    print("Shape:", img.shape)
+    print("Min:", img.min().item())
+    print("Max:", img.max().item())
+    print("Mean:", img.mean().item())
+    print("Finite:", torch.isfinite(img).all().item())
+
+# 6. 生成结果
+output_dir = Path("results/R03_forward")
+output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+
+save_image(
+    reference_rgb,
+    output_dir / "reference_rgb.png"
+)
+save_image(
+    encoded_rgb,
+    output_dir / "encoded_rgb.png"
+)
+save_image(
+    torch.cat([reference_rgb, encoded_rgb], dim=-1),
+    output_dir / "comparison.png"
+)
+
+difference = (
+    reference_rgb - encoded_rgb
+).abs().mean().item()
+
+print("RGB 平均绝对差异:", difference)
+print("结果保存到:", output_dir.resolve())
+print("R03 前向成像完成！")
diff --git a/3_test_pretrained.py b/3_test_pretrained.py
index e69de29..9adafbf 100644
--- a/3_test_pretrained.py
+++ b/3_test_pretrained.py
@@ -0,0 +1,179 @@
+
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import torch
+import yaml
+from torchvision.utils import save_image
+
+from hsi_dataset import CaveDataset
+from src.hsi_camera import HSICamera
+from src.network import NAFNet
+
+device = torch.device(
+    "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
+)
+
+# 1. 加载原仓库配置
+with open("configs/1_deeplens_hsi.yml", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+    cfg = yaml.safe_load(f)
+
+# 2. 加载一个 CAVE 验证场景
+dataset = CaveDataset(
+    file_path=cfg["eval_set"]["dataset"],
+    crop_size=(256, 256),
+    is_train=False
+)
+sample = dataset[0]
+
+data = {
+    "wvln": sample["wvln"].unsqueeze(0),
+    "img": sample["img"].unsqueeze(0),
+}
+
+# 3. 初始化光学相机
+camera = HSICamera(
+    lens_file=cfg["camera"]["lens_file"],
+    sensor_file=cfg["camera"]["sensor_file"],
+    device=device
+)
+
+print("开始生成 DOE 编码图像...", flush=True)
+
+with torch.no_grad():
+    encoded_rgb, gt = camera.render(data)
+
+# 4. 初始化 NAFNet
+net_cfg = cfg["network"]
+
+model = NAFNet(
+    in_chan=net_cfg["in_chan"],
+    out_chan=net_cfg["out_chan"],
+    width=net_cfg["width"],
+    middle_blk_num=net_cfg["middle_blk_num"],
+    enc_blk_nums=net_cfg["enc_blk_nums"],
+    dec_blk_nums=net_cfg["dec_blk_nums"],
+).to(device)
+
+# 5. 加载预训练权重
+ckpt_path = Path(net_cfg["ckpt_path"])
+assert ckpt_path.is_file(), f"权重不存在: {ckpt_path}"
+
+weights = torch.load(
+    ckpt_path,
+    map_location=device,
+    weights_only=True
+)
+
+if isinstance(weights, dict) and "model" in weights:
+    weights = weights["model"]
+
+model.load_state_dict(weights, strict=True)
+model.eval()
+
+print("预训练权重加载成功")
+
+# 6. 网络重建
+with torch.no_grad():
+    reconstructed = model(encoded_rgb).clamp(0, 1)
+
+    reference_rgb = camera.spectral2rgb(gt)
+    reconstructed_rgb = camera.spectral2rgb(reconstructed)
+
+# 7. 计算光谱重建 PSNR
+mse = torch.mean((reconstructed - gt) ** 2)
+psnr = -10 * torch.log10(mse.clamp_min(1e-12))
+
+print("编码图像:", encoded_rgb.shape)
+print("重建光谱:", reconstructed.shape)
+print("光谱重建 MSE:", mse.item())
+print("光谱重建 PSNR:", psnr.item(), "dB")
+
+# 各波段 MSE
+band_mse = ((reconstructed - gt) ** 2).mean(
+    dim=(0, 2, 3)
+)
+
+# 各波段 PSNR
+band_psnr = -10 * torch.log10(
+    band_mse.clamp_min(1e-12)
+)
+
+print("\n===== 各波段重建 PSNR =====")
+
+for i in range(31):
+    wavelength = 400 + i * 10
+    print(
+        f"{wavelength} nm: "
+        f"{band_psnr[i].item():.2f} dB"
+    )
+
+# 检查重建 RGB 的颜色偏差
+print("\n===== RGB 通道平均值 =====")
+
+for name, image in [
+    ("Reference", reference_rgb),
+    ("Reconstructed", reconstructed_rgb)
+]:
+    means = image.mean(dim=(0, 2, 3))
+    print(name, means.cpu().tolist())
+
+# 8. 保存可视化结果
+out = Path("results/R04_pretrained")
+out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+
+comparison = torch.cat(
+    [reference_rgb, encoded_rgb, reconstructed_rgb],
+    dim=3
+)
+
+save_image(
+    comparison.clamp(0, 1),
+    out / "comparison.png"
+)
+
+torch.save(
+    reconstructed.cpu(),
+    out / "reconstructed_hsi.pt"
+)
+
+print("R04 推理完成！")
+print("结果路径:", out.resolve())
+
+print("\n===== 波段重建能力诊断 =====")
+
+# 真实光谱各波段平均强度
+band_mean = gt.mean(dim=(0, 2, 3))
+
+# 各波段重建 MSE
+model_mse = ((reconstructed - gt) ** 2).mean(
+    dim=(0, 2, 3)
+)
+
+# 零图像基准：始终预测零
+zero_mse = (gt ** 2).mean(dim=(0, 2, 3))
+
+model_psnr = -10 * torch.log10(
+    model_mse.clamp_min(1e-12)
+)
+
+zero_psnr = -10 * torch.log10(
+    zero_mse.clamp_min(1e-12)
+)
+
+for i in [0, 5, 10, 15, 20, 25, 30]:
+    print(
+        f"{400 + i * 10} nm | "
+        f"GT均值={band_mean[i].item():.5f} | "
+        f"模型PSNR={model_psnr[i].item():.2f} | "
+        f"零图基准={zero_psnr[i].item():.2f} | "
+        f"提升={(model_psnr[i]-zero_psnr[i]).item():.2f} dB"
+    )
+
+print("\n===== RGB通道平均值 =====")
+
+for name, img in [
+    ("Reference", reference_rgb),
+    ("Reconstructed", reconstructed_rgb)
+]:
+    means = img.mean(dim=(0, 2, 3))
+    print(name, means.cpu().tolist())

+
+import cv2
+import numpy as np
+from pathlib import Path
+
+# 选择一个实际波段
+folder = Path(
+    "datasets/cave/photo_and_face_ms/photo_and_face_ms"
+)
+path = folder / "photo_and_face_ms_01.png"
+
+# 原始 16 位读取
+img16 = cv2.imread(str(path), cv2.IMREAD_UNCHANGED)
+
+# 原仓库 8 位读取
+img8 = cv2.imread(str(path), cv2.IMREAD_GRAYSCALE)
+
+# 两种归一化方式
+x16 = img16.astype(np.float64) / 65535.0
+x8 = img8.astype(np.float64) / 255.0
+
+error = np.abs(x16 - x8)
+
+print("原始类型:", img16.dtype)
+print("仓库读取类型:", img8.dtype)
+
+print("\n===== 归一化强度 =====")
+print("16位均值:", x16.mean())
+print("8位均值:", x8.mean())
+
+print("\n===== 量化误差 =====")
+print("平均绝对误差:", error.mean())
+print("最大绝对误差:", error.max())
+
+print("\n===== 灰度等级数量 =====")
+print("原始16位:", len(np.unique(img16)))
+print("转换8位:", len(np.unique(img8)))
diff --git a/5_eval_pretrained.py b/5_eval_pretrained.py
index e69de29..57442b5 100644
--- a/5_eval_pretrained.py
+++ b/5_eval_pretrained.py
@@ -0,0 +1,139 @@
+
+import argparse
+import time
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import torch
+import yaml
+from torchvision.utils import save_image
+
+from hsi_dataset import CaveDataset
+from src.hsi_camera import HSICamera
+from src.network import NAFNet
+
+
+def main():
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
+    parser.add_argument("--limit", type=int, default=1)
+    args = parser.parse_args()
+
+    assert torch.cuda.is_available(), "未检测到 CUDA GPU"
+    device = torch.device("cuda")
+
+    # 1. 加载原仓库配置
+    with open("configs/1_deeplens_hsi.yml", "r",
+              encoding="utf-8") as f:
+        cfg = yaml.safe_load(f)
+
+    # 2. 验证集：保持原仓库的 512×512 配置
+    dataset = CaveDataset(
+        file_path=cfg["eval_set"]["dataset"],
+        crop_size=cfg["eval_set"]["crop_size"],
+        is_train=False
+    )
+
+    print("验证集场景总数:", len(dataset))
+    print("本次测试场景数:", min(args.limit, len(dataset)))
+    print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(0))
+
+    # 3. 初始化 DOE 相机
+    camera = HSICamera(
+        lens_file=cfg["camera"]["lens_file"],
+        sensor_file=cfg["camera"]["sensor_file"],
+        device=device
+    )
+
+    # 4. 初始化 NAFNet
+    nc = cfg["network"]
+    model = NAFNet(
+        in_chan=nc["in_chan"],
+        out_chan=nc["out_chan"],
+        width=nc["width"],
+        middle_blk_num=nc["middle_blk_num"],
+        enc_blk_nums=nc["enc_blk_nums"],
+        dec_blk_nums=nc["dec_blk_nums"]
+    ).to(device)
+
+    ckpt = torch.load(
+        nc["ckpt_path"],
+        map_location=device,
+        weights_only=True
+    )
+
+    if isinstance(ckpt, dict) and "model" in ckpt:
+        ckpt = ckpt["model"]
+
+    model.load_state_dict(ckpt, strict=True)
+    model.eval()
+
+    print("预训练模型加载成功")
+
+    # 5. 创建输出目录
+    out_root = Path("results/R05_validation")
+    out_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+
+    # 6. 逐场景前向编码与重建
+    for i in range(min(args.limit, len(dataset))):
+        start = time.perf_counter()
+        scene_name = Path(dataset.image_folders[i]).name
+        out_dir = out_root / scene_name
+        out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+
+        print(f"\n===== 场景 {i + 1}: {scene_name} =====",
+              flush=True)
+
+        sample = dataset[i]
+
+        data = {
+            "wvln": sample["wvln"].unsqueeze(0),
+            "img": sample["img"].unsqueeze(0)
+        }
+
+        with torch.no_grad():
+            # 完整原仓库 DOE 前向模型
+            encoded_rgb, gt = camera.render(data)
+
+            # 网络重建 31 波段
+            pred = model(encoded_rgb).clamp(0, 1)
+
+            # RGB 可视化
+            reference_rgb = camera.spectral2rgb(gt)
+            reconstructed_rgb = camera.spectral2rgb(pred)
+
+            # 计算当前场景的总体 PSNR
+            mse = torch.mean((pred - gt) ** 2)
+            psnr = -10 * torch.log10(
+                mse.clamp_min(1e-12)
+            )
+
+        # 7. 保存结果
+        comparison = torch.cat(
+            [reference_rgb, encoded_rgb, reconstructed_rgb],
+            dim=3
+        )
+
+        save_image(
+            comparison.clamp(0, 1),
+            out_dir / "comparison.png"
+        )
+
+        # 保存光谱真值和预测，用于后续指标评价
+        torch.save(
+            {
+                "gt": gt.detach().cpu(),
+                "pred": pred.detach().cpu()
+            },
+            out_dir / "spectra.pt"
+        )
+
+        print("GT shape:", tuple(gt.shape))
+        print("Pred shape:", tuple(pred.shape))
+        print("PSNR:", round(psnr.item(), 4), "dB")
+        print("耗时:", round(time.perf_counter() - start, 1), "秒")
+        print("保存路径:", out_dir.resolve(), flush=True)
+
+    print("\nR05-2 本次推理完成")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()
diff --git a/6_evaluate_metrics.py b/6_evaluate_metrics.py
index e69de29..0c6314a 100644
--- a/6_evaluate_metrics.py
+++ b/6_evaluate_metrics.py
@@ -0,0 +1,112 @@
+
+import csv
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import torch
+
+from src.utils import batch_ssim
+
+
+root = Path("results/R05_validation")
+files = sorted(root.glob("*/spectra.pt"))
+
+assert len(files) == 8, (
+    f"预期8个场景，实际找到{len(files)}个"
+)
+
+
+def compute_metrics(pred, gt):
+    assert pred.shape == gt.shape
+    assert pred.ndim == 4 and pred.shape[1] == 31
+    assert torch.isfinite(pred).all()
+    assert torch.isfinite(gt).all()
+
+    # 1. PSNR: 与此前实验保持一致
+    mse = ((pred - gt) ** 2).mean()
+    psnr = -10 * torch.log10(
+        mse.clamp_min(1e-12)
+    )
+
+    # 2. SSIM: 采用原仓库实现
+    # 内部转换为8位图像后计算
+    ssim = batch_ssim(pred, gt)
+
+    # 3. SAM: 每个像素沿31个光谱通道计算夹角
+    dot = (pred * gt).sum(dim=1)
+    gt_norm = torch.linalg.vector_norm(gt, dim=1)
+    pred_norm = torch.linalg.vector_norm(pred, dim=1)
+
+    # 零向量的光谱角无定义，将其排除
+    valid = (gt_norm > 1e-8) & (pred_norm > 1e-8)
+
+    assert valid.any(), "无有效光谱像素"
+
+    cosine = dot[valid] / (
+        gt_norm[valid] * pred_norm[valid]
+    )
+    cosine = cosine.clamp(-1.0, 1.0)
+
+    angles_deg = torch.rad2deg(
+        torch.acos(cosine)
+    )
+    sam = angles_deg.mean()
+    valid_pct = valid.float().mean() * 100
+
+    return {
+        "psnr_db": float(psnr),
+        "ssim": float(ssim),
+        "sam_deg": float(sam),
+        "sam_valid_pct": float(valid_pct),
+    }
+
+
+rows = []
+
+for path in files:
+    data = torch.load(
+        path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True
+    )
+    gt = data["gt"].float()
+    pred = data["pred"].float()
+
+    result = compute_metrics(pred, gt)
+    result["scene"] = path.parent.name
+    rows.append(result)
+
+    print(
+        f'{result["scene"]:30s} | '
+        f'PSNR={result["psnr_db"]:.2f} dB | '
+        f'SSIM={result["ssim"]:.4f} | '
+        f'SAM={result["sam_deg"]:.2f} deg'
+    )
+
+# 逐场景算术平均
+print("\n===== 验证集平均指标 =====")
+
+for key in ["psnr_db", "ssim", "sam_deg"]:
+    avg = sum(r[key] for r in rows) / len(rows)
+    print(f"{key}: {avg:.4f}")
+
+print(
+    "SAM有效像素比例范围:",
+    f'{min(r["sam_valid_pct"] for r in rows):.2f}%',
+    "至",
+    f'{max(r["sam_valid_pct"] for r in rows):.2f}%'
+)
+
+# 保存评价表格
+output = root / "metrics.csv"
+
+with open(output, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8-sig") as f:
+    writer = csv.DictWriter(
+        f,
+        fieldnames=[
+            "scene", "psnr_db", "ssim",
+            "sam_deg", "sam_valid_pct"
+        ]
+    )
+    writer.writeheader()
+    writer.writerows(rows)
+
+print("\n评价结果已保存:", output.resolve())
+print("R05-3 完成")

 This repository provides code for end-to-end hyperspectral imaging (HSI) simulation and reconstruction using the [DeepLens](https://github.com/singer-yang/DeepLens) framework. It demonstrates how to model diffractive optical elements (DOEs) for HSI and train deep learning models to recover spectral information from simulated RGB sensor captures.

+![HSI Reconstruction Demo](./assets/video1_raw.gif)
